# Seed 37 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the new genes' first run: memory moves (Iteration 126)

`seed 37 tag2 9h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit d5917cd (Iteration 123), started 03:24: the fronts of
seeds 32, 33, 35 and 36 carried in (604 designs), four workers (cpus 2, 12,
14, 4), population 256, 165 generations before the deadline (7 h 41 min of
evolution), 42,225 designs, 40,132 alive; 2,092 refused by the reach limit.
The first run of the genes new since seed 35 - fbuf, kstack, abortif, opcall
- and of the stack guard fixed. The chain took 9 h 08 min: the seed pack 8 h
05 min in (the front re-measured in 22 min 41 s), the comparison 63 min 40
s; Iteration 124 had estimated 9 h 10-30 - the serial re-measure and a larger
session outgrow the 80-minute reserve. Session 32 measured it with seeds 32,
33, 35 and 36 (calibration 1.003; cpu 2 1.003, cpu 8 1.009): with
--frontier, 422 designs in 64 minutes.

**Not small: memory moved.** Against the best of the earlier runs in the
session:

| objective | seed 37 | seeds 32-36, best | |
|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.134 | 0.133 (seed 33) | no gain |
| smallest binary + image | 20,430 | 20,468 (seed 35) | 0.19% smaller, under 2% |
| least memory | 156 KB | 184 KB (seed 35) | 28 KB less, past the 8 KB tie |

As predicted before the run (Iteration 124): with fbuf on the front, memory
falls past the tie, and that alone makes the run not small. **On the front of
all runs (107): 90 of seed 37** - against 11 of seed 35, 4 of seed 33, 2 of
seed 32, none of seed 36.

The new genes on its front (242 designs, 238 bred in this run): **fbuf on 123**
(121 with 1 KB buffers, 2 with 512 B) - those at 152-184 KB, the 119 without
at 184-220 KB, among them the fastest (0.125 in the run): the front holds
memory against speed; **abortif on 79**; **kstack on 17** (expressed only
without kinput, which 223 have); **opcall on 13**, one operation each. The
rest as before: the tag on all, kfast 240, opbody 229, kinput 223, t2wide 196,
alignfn 175, hotinl 172, noinls 160, klookup 159, tracer 112, rtfuse 69, the
JIT 60, the guard 58.

The stopping rule: the new genes start the count afresh, and seed 37 is not
small - none in a row.

**The held-out check** (lab/evolve/ans-check.py, the Forth 2012 test suite,
at Iteration 125's code - every image built with RelF's fixes): **all 242
front designs say what s6 says on all 14 files**, the 58 with the guard among
them; the negative control caught in both batches of 121.

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

42225 designs evaluated, 40132 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
